# 2026-10-10 Г?" FIX-154 trusted higher-timeframe BUY assessment (implementation)

- **Problem:** every bearish 1h row had equal veto authority, including
  historical rows with unknown data-quality provenance; there was no durable
  explanation of the effective 5m/1h BUY context.
- **Root cause:** BUY validation and consolidation read raw `TradeSignal`
  decisions directly, with no assessment of quality or bearish strength.
- **Solution:** added `data_quality_valid` persistence to `TradeSignal` and
  `AnalysisService`; introduced `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`; wired it
  into direct BUY, accumulated-evidence authority, consolidation and
  observational progressive-add evidence. The SOFT 1h result is capped at 25%
  and uses immutable effective decisions; HARD and unknown bearish evidence
  vetoes. `V107` creates `data_quality_valid` and append-only
  `buy_htf_assessment_audit`.
- **Files/classes:** `TradeSignal`, `AnalysisService`,
  `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`, `TradeExecutionValidationService`,
  `OpportunityConsolidationService`, `ExecutionIntelligenceService`,
  `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore`, `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql`,
  and focused tests.
- **Database impact:** new nullable `trade_signal.data_quality_valid`; no
  backfill. Historical `NULL` stays unknown. New append-only audit table.
- **Production / Replay impact:** the same shared validation service is used
  for both. Audit failure is explicitly non-blocking. V108 ships because live
  runtime outcome writers require its schema; reconciliation remains disabled.
- **Validation status:** focused Maven tests could not run in this shell because
  the active JDK reports `release version 21 not supported`; execute with the
  project Java 21 toolchain before release.

## Validation correction Г?" 2026-10-10

(`C:\Program Files\Java\jdk-21`). Focused Maven verification then passed:
`HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`,
`TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`, and
`OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`.

## FIX-154 review-round correction Г?" 2026-10-10

- **Replay parity:** V107 now brings `trade_signal_test` forward with the
  V97 BTC freshness fields, V106 `signal_mode`, and FIX-154
  `data_quality_valid`, matching the reflection-based Replay writer.
- **Audit isolation:** audit records are snapshot and submitted to a bounded,
  one-thread executor. Replay never writes a Production audit row, and audit
  saturation/failure only increments an in-memory metric.
- **Evidence accuracy:** audit candle close times are derived from candle open
  time plus the interval, hard-confirmation is persisted, and both legacy veto
  codes are retained.

  FIX-154 verifies its required production and replay columns before runtime
  admission.

  matrix, validation, consolidation and early outcome-schema verifier tests.

## FIX-153 automatic reconciliation enabled Г?" 2026-10-10

- Problem: a missing historical candle left current candle ingestion healthy
  but blocked later signal generation for the affected lane.
- Root cause: the data-quality gate correctly rejects incomplete windows; the
  recovery component was present but disabled.
- Solution: enabled OFF-mode token-free reconciliation, kept its one-worker
  executor as the default, and added deterministic tests for reconnect

  `LocalCandleReconciliationServiceTest`.
- Database impact: uses existing V108 audit/outcome tables; no data rewrite or
  backfill was executed by this change.
- Production/Replay impact: recovery is non-executable and has no Replay
  authority. Targeted Java 21 reconciliation tests passed; real-MySQL release
  tests still require the disposable test environment.

  `Fix154MigrationContractTest` and `LocalCandleReconciliationServiceTest`

### codex-docs/TRADE_INVESTIGATION_AGENT.md
# Trade Investigation Agent

## Role

Investigate one Crypto AI Trader trade from durable evidence. Explain what
happened, why the system entered and exited, whether it followed the intended
rules, and whether the entry/exit was supported by the information available
at that time.

Investigations are read-only. Do not change code, configuration, services,
database records, review states, Replay work, or deployment state.

## Required input

Obtain or resolve:

- Environment and application: local, test, or production.
- Database host, port, and schema.
- One trade reference: `paper_position.id`, `wallet_trade.id`, or a symbol plus
  a bounded approximate entry-time window.
- Optional question or suspected issue.

Before analysis, confirm the symbol, trade/position ID, running version or FIX
level, and ingestion mode. Read the actual schema before choosing tables or
columns. Do not assume a table is in `crypto_ai` when it may belong to
`crypto_ai_v2`.

## Evidence rules

- Use configured database and SSH/log connections without displaying secrets.
- Use bounded, read-only queries and targeted log searches.
- Treat stored timestamps as UTC; display both UTC and KSA (`UTC+03:00`).
- Attach record IDs and timestamped log evidence to every factual conclusion.
- Distinguish **confirmed fact**, **supported inference**, and **missing or
  conflicting evidence**.
- Missing logs or records do not prove that no execution/effect occurred.
- A saved signal does not prove execution. A callback return does not prove a
  transaction committed. Under FIX-153, `market_price_event.APPLIED` proves a
  committed successful protection callback, but can be a no-op when there was
  no open position; find the position/trade mutation separately.
- Current configuration does not prove which settings were active at entry. If
  no configuration history exists, report that limitation.

## Investigation workflow

### 1. Resolve identity and environment

Link the trade reference to, where available:

- `paper_position`
- `wallet_trade`
- entry and exit `trade_signal`
- `signal_processing_work`
- wallet/position execution records

Record entry and exit times in UTC and KSA. Confirm whether the trader ran in
local-candle OFF mode or a legacy shared-market mode.

### 2. Collect a small evidence bundle

Start with exact IDs and a narrow time window. Expand only when the evidence
requires it. Collect:

| Evidence | Establishes |
| --- | --- |
| `paper_position` | lifecycle, entry/exit, stop, target, status |
| `wallet_trade` | executed quantity, prices, fees, linked signal |
| `trade_signal` | decision, strategy, scores, entry eligibility, reasons |
| `signal_processing_work` | claim, attempt, completion, expiry, uncertainty |
| `candle` / `technical_indicator` | market data and analysis inputs available then |
| `market_price_event` | intra-candle protection observations and outcomes |
| configuration/version evidence | applicable rules, when historically available |
| application and timing logs | analysis, execution, protection, exceptions, waits |

Avoid fetching large signal explanation fields until they are needed.

### 3. Reconstruct the timeline

Build a timeline, explicitly showing missing stages:

`candle closed Г+' signal saved Г+' work claimed Г+' locks acquired Г+' entry committed Г+' protection observations Г+' exit decision Г+' exit committed`

Measure separately:

- candle close to signal generation;
- signal generation to processing/entry;
- observation time to protection completion;
- exit-trigger evidence to recorded exit.

Correlate delays only with logs/events for the same symbol, position, signal,
or price-event identity. A nearby slow SQL query is not proof of causation.

### 4. Assess entry correctness and quality

Use only evidence that was available at entry. Check:

- signal mode, closed-candle identity, freshness, and data quality;
- final decision and entry-allowed result;
- BTC context, higher-timeframe, ATR, liquidity, risk/reward, sizing, and
  strategy gates that applied at that time;
- correct signal/position linkage and absence of duplicate/stale-retry entry;
- quantity, wallet balance, risk limits, fees, and fill/reference-price gap.

Return two separate conclusions:

- **Execution correctness:** followed or violated the implemented rules.
- **Entry quality:** supported, weak, or unverifiable from contemporaneous
  evidence.

Subsequent price movement may evaluate the outcome; it must never be presented
as information the entry decision had.

### 5. Reconstruct protection and stop-loss behavior

For FIX-153, inspect accepted `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` price events and retain
`PENDING`, `NON_CANONICAL`, and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` records as evidence
gaps or exclusions. Never substitute candle OHLC for absent intra-candle price
evidence.

For each relevant accepted observation:

1. Determine the then-active stop, target, trailing stop, and profit-lock state.
2. Find the first accepted observation that met an exit threshold.
3. Confirm that protection evaluated it and identify the resulting position or
   wallet execution record.
4. Explain delays, rejections, stale/out-of-order classification, exceptions,
   lock waits, or pool pressure.

Classify the exit path accurately: fixed stop-loss, trailing stop, profit-lock,
take-profit, signal-driven exit, manual exit, or unknown.

### 6. Assess exit correctness and quality

Compare the recorded exit reason, quantity, and price with trigger evidence.
Check threshold reach, accepted-observation identity, protection timing, and
the committed execution. Calculate maximum favourable/adverse movement during
the holding period separately from the contemporaneous exit assessment.

### 7. Reconcile financial results

Recalculate using execution records:

- bought/sold quantities and weighted entry/exit prices;
- gross P&L, fees, and net P&L;
- partial exits, remaining quantity, and realized versus unrealized values.

Flag discrepancies among wallet records, position records, and UI output.

### 8. Diagnose cause and use Replay carefully

Classify findings as one or more of:

- strategy decision;
- data freshness or coverage;
- analysis delay;
- execution failure;
- protection failure;
- database, lock, or connection-pool contention;
- accounting/UI discrepancy;
- insufficient evidence.

Run Replay only as a controlled comparison with equivalent candles, accepted
prices, strategy version, and settings. State every unavailable input or
material difference. Never claim parity by substituting candle OHLC for missing
price evidence or treating `PENDING` as accepted.

## Required final report

1. **Trade identity and environment** Г?" IDs, symbol, version/mode, scope.
2. **UTC/KSA timeline** Г?" evidence-backed sequence and latency breakdown.
3. **Entry** Г?" execution correctness and entry quality.
4. **Exit** Г?" execution correctness and exit quality.
5. **Stop-loss/profit-lock** Г?" worked, failed, or unverifiable, with evidence.
6. **P&L reconciliation** Г?" quantities, prices, fees, and discrepancies.
7. **Root cause and confidence** Г?" confirmed defect, valid loss, configuration
   issue, delay, or insufficient evidence.
8. **Evidence references** Г?" record IDs, query/log timestamps, code/config
   references.
9. **Missing evidence** Г?" exactly what prevents a stronger conclusion.
10. **Recommended correction and regression checks** Г?" only when justified.

A profitable trade can violate the rules, and a losing trade can be a correct
execution. State those independently.

### src/main/java/com/crypto/service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java
1:package com.crypto.service;
2:
3:import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
4:import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
5:import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
6:
7:/**
8: * Pure FIX-154 BUY-context assessment. It deliberately returns effective
9: * decisions separately and never mutates the JPA-managed context signals.
10: */
11:@Service
12:public class HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService {
13:    public static final int HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 70;
14:
15:    public Assessment assess(TradeSignal fiveMinute, boolean fiveFresh,
16:                             TradeSignal oneHour, boolean oneFresh) {
17:        if (!fiveFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
18:        if (!oneFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
19:
20:        SignalDecision five = decision(fiveMinute);
21:        SignalDecision one = decision(oneHour);
22:        if (bearish(five)) {
23:            boolean hard = hard(oneHour);
24:            return Assessment.reject("5M_BEARISH_VETO", hard ? Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE : band(oneHour), fiveMinute, oneHour)
25:                    .withHardConfirmation(hard);
26:        }
27:        if (bearish(one) && !qualityValid(oneHour)) {
28:            return Assessment.reject("1H_BEARISH_VETO", Band.NOT_TRUSTED, fiveMinute, oneHour);
29:        }
30:        if (hard(oneHour)) {
31:            return Assessment.reject("HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO", Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
32:        }
33:        if (soft(oneHour)) {
34:            return Assessment.allow(Band.SOFT_CONFLICT, five, SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, 25, fiveMinute, oneHour);
35:        }
36:        return Assessment.allow(qualityValid(oneHour) ? Band.NO_CONFLICT : Band.NOT_TRUSTED,
37:                five, one, null, fiveMinute, oneHour);
38:    }
39:
40:    private Band band(TradeSignal one) {
41:        if (!qualityValid(one)) return Band.NOT_TRUSTED;
42:        if (hard(one)) return Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE;
43:        if (soft(one)) return Band.SOFT_CONFLICT;
44:        return Band.NO_CONFLICT;
45:    }
46:    private boolean hard(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) >= HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD; }
47:    private boolean soft(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && (decision(s) == SignalDecision.SELL || (decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) < HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD)); }
48:    private boolean qualityValid(TradeSignal s) { return s != null && Boolean.TRUE.equals(s.getDataQualityValid()); }
49:    private int confidence(TradeSignal s) { return s == null ? 0 : s.getConfidenceScore(); }
50:    private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
51:    private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.SELL || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL; }
52:
53:    public enum Band { NOT_APPLICABLE, NOT_TRUSTED, SOFT_CONFLICT, HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, NO_CONFLICT }
54:    public record Assessment(boolean allowed, String code, Band band, SignalDecision effectiveFiveMinute,
55:                             SignalDecision effectiveOneHour, Integer maxInitialPercent,
56:                             boolean hardConfirmationPresent, TradeSignal rawFiveMinute, TradeSignal rawOneHour) {
57:        static Assessment reject(String code, Band band, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
58:            return new Assessment(false, code, band, decisionOf(five), decisionOf(one), null, false, five, one);
59:        }
60:        static Assessment allow(Band band, SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one, Integer cap, TradeSignal rawFive, TradeSignal rawOne) {
61:            return new Assessment(true, "HTF_CONTEXT_ALLOWED", band, five, one, cap, false, rawFive, rawOne);
62:        }
63:        Assessment withHardConfirmation(boolean value) { return new Assessment(allowed, code, band, effectiveFiveMinute, effectiveOneHour, maxInitialPercent, value, rawFiveMinute, rawOneHour); }
64:        private static SignalDecision decisionOf(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
65:    }
66:}

### src/main/java/com/crypto/service/MarketContextService.java
1:package com.crypto.service;
2:
3:import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
4:import com.crypto.dto.AtrRiskAssessment;
5:import com.crypto.dto.BtcMarketContextResult;
6:import com.crypto.dto.IndicatorSnapshot;
7:import com.crypto.dto.MarketContextSnapshot;
8:import com.crypto.dto.MultiTimeframeConfluenceResult;
9:import com.crypto.dto.OrderBookLiquidityResult;
10:import com.crypto.dto.DerivativesPositioningResult;
11:import com.crypto.dto.ProviderSentiment;
12:import com.crypto.dto.SentimentOverview;
13:import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
14:import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
15:
16:import java.math.BigDecimal;
17:import java.math.RoundingMode;
18:import java.time.Instant;
19:import java.util.ArrayList;
20:import java.util.List;
21:
22:/**
23: * Builds the complete market context before strategy selection.  Directional
24: * services are queried with NEUTRAL so they expose context without vetoing a
25: * decision that has not yet been calculated.
26: */
27:@Service
28:@RequiredArgsConstructor
29:public class MarketContextService {
30:
31:    private final MultiTimeframeConfluenceService multiTimeframeConfluenceService;
32:    private final BtcMarketContextService btcMarketContextService;
33:    private final OrderBookLiquidityService orderBookLiquidityService;
34:    private final DerivativesPositioningService derivativesPositioningService;
35:
36:    public MarketContextSnapshot build(
37:            IndicatorSnapshot indicator,
38:            AtrRiskAssessment atrRisk,
39:            SentimentOverview sentimentOverview,
40:            boolean sentimentEnabled,
41:            Instant evaluatedAt,
42:            boolean historicalReplay
43:    ) {
44:        Instant snapshotTime = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
45:        MultiTimeframeConfluenceResult timeframe = multiTimeframeConfluenceService.evaluate(
46:                indicator.symbol(), indicator.intervalCode(), SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, snapshotTime);
47:        BtcMarketContextResult btc = btcMarketContextService.evaluate(
48:                indicator.symbol(), indicator.intervalCode(), SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, true, snapshotTime);
49:        OrderBookLiquidityResult liquidity = historicalReplay
50:                ? orderBookLiquidityService.evaluateHistorical(
51:                        indicator.symbol(), indicator.intervalCode(), SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, true,
52:                        indicator.latestPrice(), atrRisk.stopLoss(), atrRisk.takeProfit(), snapshotTime)
53:                : orderBookLiquidityService.evaluate(
54:                        indicator.symbol(), indicator.intervalCode(), SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, true, indicator.latestPrice(),
55:                        atrRisk.stopLoss(), atrRisk.takeProfit(), snapshotTime);
56:        DerivativesPositioningResult derivatives = historicalReplay
57:                ? derivativesPositioningService.evaluateHistorical(
58:                        indicator.symbol(), indicator.intervalCode(), SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, true, snapshotTime)
59:                : derivativesPositioningService.evaluate(
60:                        indicator.symbol(), indicator.intervalCode(), SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, true, snapshotTime);
61:
62:        List<ProviderSentiment> providers = sentimentOverview == null || sentimentOverview.providers() == null
63:                ? List.of() : sentimentOverview.providers();
64:        int enabled = (int) providers.stream().filter(ProviderSentiment::enabled).count();
65:        int contributing = (int) providers.stream()
66:                .filter(ProviderSentiment::enabled)
67:                .filter(provider -> provider.effectiveWeight() != null && provider.effectiveWeight().signum() > 0)
68:                .count();
69:        BigDecimal coverage = enabled == 0
70:                ? (sentimentEnabled ? BigDecimal.ZERO : BigDecimal.ONE)
71:                : BigDecimal.valueOf(contributing)
72:                    .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(enabled), 4, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
73:
74:        boolean valid = indicator.latestPrice() != null && indicator.latestPrice().signum() > 0
75:                && indicator.ema20() != null && indicator.ema50() != null && indicator.ema200() != null;
76:        List<String> evidence = new ArrayList<>();
77:        evidence.add("Higher-timeframe context: " + timeframe.status());
78:        evidence.add("BTC relationship/context: " + btc.relationshipType() + " / " + btc.contextStatus());
79:        evidence.add("Order-book liquidity: " + liquidity.status());
80:        evidence.add("Funding/open interest: " + derivatives.status());
81:        if (sentimentEnabled) {
82:            evidence.add("Sentiment coverage: " + contributing + "/" + enabled + " enabled providers");
83:        } else {
84:            evidence.add("Sentiment is disabled and removed from the available maximum");
85:        }
86:        if (!valid) {
87:            evidence.add("Required technical inputs are incomplete");
88:        }
89:
90:        return new MarketContextSnapshot(
91:                indicator.symbol(), indicator.intervalCode(), snapshotTime, valid,
92:                timeframe.status(), timeframe.higherInterval(), timeframe.higherTimeframeTrendScore(),
93:                btc.relationshipType(), btc.contextStatus(), btc.correlation(), btc.beta(),
94:                btc.influenceFactor(), btc.stable(), liquidity.status(), liquidity.imbalance(),
95:                liquidity.spreadPercent(), liquidity.observations(), derivatives.status(),
96:                derivatives.fundingRate(), derivatives.fundingPercentile(),
97:                derivatives.openInterestChangePercent(), derivatives.priceChangePercent(),
98:                derivatives.confidenceAdjustment(), sentimentEnabled,
99:                enabled, contributing, coverage, List.copyOf(evidence));
100:    }
101:}

### src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/StopLossEvidenceService.java
1:package com.crypto.execution.service;
2:
3:import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
4:import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision;
5:import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionIntelligenceService.SetupWakeupEvaluation;
6:import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
7:import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
8:import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
9:import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
10:import java.time.Instant;
11:import java.time.Clock;
12:import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
13:import java.util.*;
14:import java.util.function.Supplier;
15:
16:/** FIX-122: evaluation-local history selection. Spring singleton state is thread-bound
17: * and restored in finally; open-position additions never enter this scope. Replay uses
18: * its own executed stops, never Production history. No wallet lock or balance writes.
19: */
20:@Service
21:@Slf4j
22:public class StopLossEvidenceService {
23:    private final StopLossEvidenceStore store;
24:    private final ExecutionReplayScope replay;
25:    private final ObjectMapper mapper;
26:    private final boolean enabled;
27:    private final Clock clock;
28:    private final ThreadLocal<Context> active = new ThreadLocal<>();
29:
30:    @Autowired
31:    public StopLossEvidenceService(StopLossEvidenceStore store, ExecutionReplayScope replay,
32:                                  ObjectMapper mapper, @Value("${trading.fix122.enabled:true}") boolean enabled) {
33:        this(store,replay,mapper,enabled,Clock.systemUTC());
34:    }
35:
36:    StopLossEvidenceService(StopLossEvidenceStore store, ExecutionReplayScope replay,
37:                           ObjectMapper mapper, boolean enabled, Clock clock) {
38:        this.store=store; this.replay=replay; this.mapper=mapper; this.enabled=enabled; this.clock=clock;
39:    }
40:
41:    private boolean enabled() { return replay.active() ? replay.fix122Enabled() : enabled; }
42:    private Instant now() { return replay.active() ? replay.referenceInstant() : clock.instant(); }
43:    private StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary resolve(String symbol, Instant at) {
44:        return replay.active() ? replay.stopBoundary(symbol, at) : store.latest(symbol, at);
45:    }
46:    public boolean hasBoundary() { return active.get()!=null && active.get().boundary!=null; }
47:
48:    public ExecutionDecision evaluate(TradeSignal signal, int allocation, Supplier<ExecutionDecision> action) {
49:        if (allocation>0 || signal==null || signal.getGeneratedAt()==null || !enabled()) return action.get();
50:        return scoped(signal, () -> new SetupWakeupEvaluation(signal, action.get())).decision();
51:    }
52:
53:    public SetupWakeupEvaluation wakeup(TradeSignal trigger, int allocation, Supplier<SetupWakeupEvaluation> action) {
54:        if (allocation>0 || trigger==null || trigger.getGeneratedAt()==null || !enabled()) return action.get();
55:        return scoped(trigger, action);
56:    }
57:
58:    private SetupWakeupEvaluation scoped(TradeSignal trigger, Supplier<SetupWakeupEvaluation> action) {
59:        Context previous=active.get();
60:        Context c=new Context(trigger, now(), replay.active()?replay.runId():null);
61:        active.set(c);
62:        try {
63:            try { c.boundary=resolve(trigger.getSymbol(), c.at); }
64:            catch (RuntimeException error) {
65:                log.warn("[FIX-122] BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE symbol={} signalId={}", trigger.getSymbol(), trigger.getId(), error);
66:                c.status="BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE";
67:                ExecutionDecision d=ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE",
68:                        "Stop history could not be checked. Entry deferred to the next normal evaluation.");
69:                audit(c,d,"EVALUATION");
70:                return new SetupWakeupEvaluation(trigger,d);
71:            }
72:            SetupWakeupEvaluation result;
73:            if ("1m".equals(trigger.getInterval()) && !eligible(trigger,"CURRENT_SIGNAL")) {
74:                result=new SetupWakeupEvaluation(trigger, ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_CURRENT_CANDLE_STALE",
75:                        "Current entry candle is not eligible after the completed stop-loss."));
76:            } else {
77:                try { result=action.get(); }
78:                catch (StaleCurrent ignored) {
79:                    result=new SetupWakeupEvaluation(c.current,ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_CURRENT_CANDLE_STALE",
80:                            "Wake-up timing candle is not eligible after the completed stop-loss."));
81:                }
82:            }
83:            if (result!=null && result.decision()!=null) {
84:                result=new SetupWakeupEvaluation(result.executionSignal(), result.decision()
85:                        .withStopBoundary(new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Stamp(c.boundary)));
86:            }
87:            audit(c,result==null?null:result.decision(),"EVALUATION");
88:            return result==null?SetupWakeupEvaluation.none():result;
89:        } finally {
90:            if(previous==null) active.remove(); else active.set(previous);
91:        }
92:    }
93:
94:    public void requireCurrent(TradeSignal signal) {
95:        if(active.get()!=null) active.get().current=signal;
96:        if(!eligible(signal,"CURRENT_SIGNAL")) throw new StaleCurrent();
97:    }
98:
99:    public boolean eligible(TradeSignal signal, String purpose) {
100:        Context c=active.get();
101:        if(c==null || c.boundary==null) return true;
102:        String reason=signal==null?"LINEAGE_UNRESOLVED":StopLossEvidencePolicy.exclusion(
103:                signal.getInterval(),signal.getCandleOpenTime(),signal.getGeneratedAt(),c.at,c.boundary);
104:        if(reason==null) return true;
105:        Map<String,Object> item=new LinkedHashMap<>();
106:        item.put("purpose",purpose); item.put("reason",reason);
107:        if(signal!=null) {
108:            item.put("signalId",signal.getId()); item.put("interval",signal.getInterval());
109:            item.put("candleOpenTime",String.valueOf(signal.getCandleOpenTime()));
110:            item.put("decision",String.valueOf(signal.getDecision()));
111:        }
112:        if(!c.excluded.contains(item)) c.excluded.add(item);
113:        return false;
114:    }
115:
116:    /** Read again at execution-price approval. A newly committed stop invalidates the
117:     * old approval. This is not a repair of the separate wallet transaction race.
118:     */
119:    public ExecutionDecision recheck(TradeSignal signal, ExecutionDecision approved) {
120:        if(approved==null || !approved.allowed() || approved.stopBoundary()==null || !enabled()) return approved;
121:        Context c=new Context(signal,now(),replay.active()?replay.runId():null);
122:        ExecutionDecision result=approved;
123:        try {
124:            c.boundary=resolve(signal.getSymbol(),c.at);
125:            if(!Objects.equals(approved.stopBoundary().boundary(),c.boundary)) {
126:                c.status="BOUNDARY_CHANGED";
127:                result=ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_BOUNDARY_CHANGED",
128:                        "A stop-loss boundary changed after qualification. Wait for a fresh evaluation.", approved.evidence());
129:            }
130:        } catch(RuntimeException error) {
131:            c.status="BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE";
132:            result=ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE",
133:                    "Stop history unavailable at price revalidation. Entry deferred.",approved.evidence());
134:            log.warn("[FIX-122] RECHECK_UNAVAILABLE symbol={}",signal.getSymbol(),error);
135:        }
136:        audit(c,result,"PRICE_RECHECK");
137:        return result;
138:    }
139:
140:    private void audit(Context c,ExecutionDecision d,String stage) {
141:        Map<String,Object> p=new LinkedHashMap<>();
142:        p.put("revision","FIX-122"); p.put("source",c.runId==null?"PRODUCTION":"REPLAY");
143:        p.put("status",c.status!=null?c.status:c.boundary==null?"NOT_APPLICABLE":
144:                c.excluded.isEmpty()?"APPLIED_NO_EXCLUSIONS":"APPLIED_EXCLUSIONS");
145:        p.put("signalId",c.current.getId()); p.put("candleOpenTime",String.valueOf(c.current.getCandleOpenTime()));
146:        p.put("triggerSignalId",c.trigger.getId());
147:        p.put("stopExecutionId",c.boundary==null?null:c.boundary.executionId());
148:        p.put("stopAt",c.boundary==null?null:c.boundary.executedAt().toString());
149:        p.put("excluded",c.excluded);
150:        p.put("allowed",d!=null&&d.allowed()); p.put("route",d==null?null:d.source());
151:        p.put("code",d==null?"NO_WAKEUP":d.code()); p.put("explanation",d==null?null:d.explanation());
152:        if(d!=null && d.evidence()!=null) {
153:            p.put("buyCount",d.evidence().buyCount()); p.put("watchCount",d.evidence().watchCount());
154:            p.put("evidenceScore",d.evidence().evidenceScore()); p.put("health",d.evidence().opportunityHealth());
155:            p.put("positionPercent",d.positionPercent());
156:        }
157:        try { store.append(c.runId,c.trigger.getSymbol(),c.current.getId(),c.at,stage,mapper.writeValueAsString(p)); }
158:        catch(Exception error) { log.error("[FIX-122] DIAGNOSTIC_PERSIST_FAILED symbol={} signalId={}",
159:                c.trigger.getSymbol(),c.current.getId(),error); }
160:        if(c.boundary!=null || c.status!=null) log.info(
161:                "[FIX-122] {} symbol={} signalId={} stop={} excluded={} code={} allowed={}",stage,
162:                c.trigger.getSymbol(),c.current.getId(),c.boundary,c.excluded.size(),p.get("code"),p.get("allowed"));
163:    }
164:
165:    private static final class StaleCurrent extends RuntimeException {}
166:    private static final class Context {
167:        final TradeSignal trigger; TradeSignal current; final Instant at; final Long runId;
168:        StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary boundary; String status;
169:        final List<Map<String,Object>> excluded=new ArrayList<>();
170:        Context(TradeSignal trigger,Instant at,Long runId) { this.trigger=trigger;this.current=trigger;this.at=at;this.runId=runId; }
171:    }
172:}

### src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java
1:package com.crypto.execution.service;
2:
3:import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
4:import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;
5:import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
6:
7:import java.time.Instant;
8:import java.util.ArrayList;
9:import java.util.Comparator;
10:import java.util.List;
11:import java.util.Optional;
12:import java.util.HashSet;
13:import java.util.Set;
14:import java.util.function.Consumer;
15:
16:/**
17: * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
18: *
19: * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
20: * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
21: * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
22: * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
23: */
24:@Component
25:public class ExecutionReplayScope {
26:
27:    /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
28:     * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
29:    public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
30:
31:    private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
32:
33:    public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
34:        return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
35:    }
36:
37:    public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
38:                      ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
39:        if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
40:        List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
41:                .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
42:                .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
43:                .toList();
44:        state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
45:        return new Scope();
46:    }
47:
48:    /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
49:    public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
50:        if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
51:        State s=required();
52:        StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
53:        if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
54:            s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
55:    }
56:    public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
57:        StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
58:        return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
59:    }
60:    public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
61:    public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
62:    public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
63:
64:    public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
65:    public long runId() { return required().runId; }
66:    public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
67:    public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
68:    public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
69:
70:    public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
71:
72:    /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
73:    public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
74:        State s = required();
75:        s.reference = observedAt;
76:        s.latestPrice = new ReplayPrice(symbol, observedAt, price);
77:    }
78:
79:    public Optional<ReplayPrice> latestPrice() {
80:        State s = required();
81:        return Optional.ofNullable(s.latestPrice);
82:    }
83:
84:    /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
85:    public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {
86:        return active() && signalId != null && required().consumedSignalIds.contains(signalId);
87:    }
88:
89:    /** FIX-112A: record only after that exact shadow BUY (initial or add) executes. */
90:    public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
91:        if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
92:    }
93:
94:    /**
95:     * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
96:     * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
97:     * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
98:     */
99:    public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
100:        if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
101:        State s = required();
102:        s.signals.add(signal);
103:        s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
104:    }
105:
106:    public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
107:        State s = required();
108:        Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
109:        if (ref == null) return List.of();
110:        return s.signals.stream()
111:                .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
112:                .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
113:                .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
114:                .limit(limit)
115:                .toList();
116:    }
117:
118:    public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
119:        return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
120:    }
121:
122:    /**
123:     * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
124:     * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
125:     * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
126:     * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
127:     * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
128:     */
129:    public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
130:        State s = required();
131:        if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
132:        return s.signals.stream()
133:                .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
134:                .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
135:                .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
136:                .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
137:    }
138:
139:    public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
140:        State s = required();
141:        return s.signals.stream()
142:                .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
143:                .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
144:                .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
145:    }
146:
147:    public Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> currentOpportunity(String symbol, List<String> statuses) {
148:        ExecutionOpportunity o = required().opportunity;
149:        if (o == null || !symbol.equals(o.getSymbol())) return Optional.empty();
150:        if (statuses != null && !statuses.isEmpty() && !statuses.contains(o.getStatus())) return Optional.empty();
151:        return Optional.of(o);
152:    }
153:
154:    public ExecutionOpportunity saveOpportunity(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity) {
155:        State s = required();
156:        s.opportunity = opportunity;
157:        if (s.opportunitySink != null) s.opportunitySink.accept(opportunity);
158:        return opportunity;
159:    }
160:
161:    public final class Scope implements AutoCloseable {
162:        private boolean closed;
163:        @Override public void close() {
164:            if (!closed) {
165:                state.remove();
166:                closed = true;
167:            }
168:        }
169:    }
170:
171:    private State required() {
172:        State s = state.get();
173:        if (s == null) throw new IllegalStateException("No Administration execution replay scope is active");
174:        return s;
175:    }
176:
177:    public record ReplayPrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {}
178:
179:    private static final class State {
180:        final long runId;
181:        final List<TradeSignal> signals;
182:        final Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink;
183:        final ReplayLogicMode logicMode;
184:        boolean fix122Enabled = true;
185:        final java.util.Map<String,StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary> stops = new java.util.HashMap<>();
186:        Instant reference;
187:        ExecutionOpportunity opportunity;
188:        ReplayPrice latestPrice;
189:        final Set<Long> consumedSignalIds = new HashSet<>();
190:        State(long runId, List<TradeSignal> signals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
191:            this.runId = runId;
192:            this.signals = new ArrayList<>(signals);
193:            this.opportunitySink = opportunitySink;
194:            this.logicMode = logicMode;
195:        }
196:    }
197:}

### src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java
1:package com.crypto.regression.service;
2:
3:import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
4:import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
5:import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;
6:import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionIntelligenceService;

13:import com.crypto.position.service.ProfitLockPolicy;

27:import java.math.BigDecimal;

33:import java.time.ZoneOffset;

35:import java.util.List;
36:import java.util.function.BooleanSupplier;
