101:    private void validateInvestigationCase(RegressionInvestigationCaseRequest request) {
102:        if (request == null || request.symbol() == null || request.symbol().isBlank()
103:                || request.startTime() == null || request.endTime() == null
104:                || !request.endTime().isAfter(request.startTime())) {
105:            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Each investigation case requires symbol/start/end and end must be after start.");
106:        }
107:    }
108:
109:    private String blankToNull(String value) {
110:        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? null : value.trim();
111:    }
112:
113:    /**
114:     * FIX-089: only rows that pre-date this service/JVM instance can be leftovers from a real
115:     * application restart. FIX-088 updated every PENDING/RUNNING row when ApplicationReadyEvent
116:     * fired, which could falsely mark a replay created by the current JVM as interrupted.
117:     *
118:     * Capturing the service construction time makes the distinction deterministic: a replay row
119:     * created after this instant belongs to the current application instance and must never be
120:     * touched by startup recovery, even if ApplicationReadyEvent is delivered later than expected.
121:     */
122:    private final Instant serviceInstanceStartedAt = Instant.now();
123:
124:    @EventListener(ApplicationReadyEvent.class)
125:    public void markInterruptedRunsAfterRestart() {
126:        jdbcTemplate.update("""
127:                UPDATE analysis_test_run
128:                SET status='ERROR', current_step='Interrupted by application restart',
129:                    error_message='Replay was interrupted by an actual application restart. Resume is disabled; delete test data and start a new run.',
130:                    completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
131:                WHERE status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')
132:                  AND created_at < ?
133:                """, Timestamp.from(serviceInstanceStartedAt));
134:    }
135:
136:    // FIX-088: manual Resume was removed. We intentionally do not expose a method that
137:    // mutates an existing failed run back to PENDING. Users delete test data and start a clean run.
138:
139:
140:    /**
141:     * FIX-090: request cooperative cancellation of exactly one active replay worker.
142:     * We do not mark the row ERROR here because that would be a false claim while Java is
143:     * still executing. RegressionTestWorker marks ERROR only after it actually unwinds.
144:     */
145:    public synchronized Map<String, Object> stopRun(long runId) {
146:        List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList(
147:                "SELECT id,status,current_step FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id=?", runId);
148:        if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Regression test #" + runId + " not found.");
149:        String status = String.valueOf(rows.get(0).get("status"));
150:        if (!("PENDING".equals(status) || "RUNNING".equals(status))) {
151:            return Map.of("id", runId, "status", status, "activeWorker", worker.isActive(runId),
152:                    "message", "Test is already stopped.");
153:        }
154:        boolean requested = worker.requestStop(runId);
155:        if (!requested) {
156:            // No worker in this JVM means it is already orphaned/stopped. Mark it ERROR now so
157:            // Delete Data is not blocked forever by a stale RUNNING database status.
158:            jdbcTemplate.update("""
159:                    UPDATE analysis_test_run
160:                    SET status='ERROR', current_step='Stopped by user',
161:                        error_message='Replay worker was not active in this application instance; run marked stopped.',
162:                        completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6), heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
163:                    WHERE id=? AND status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')
164:                    """, runId);
165:            return Map.of("id", runId, "status", "ERROR", "activeWorker", false,
166:                    "message", "Test had no active worker and is now stopped. You can Delete Data.");
167:        }
168:        jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE analysis_test_run SET current_step='Stop requested ƒ?" waiting for replay worker to exit', heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?", runId);
169:        return Map.of("id", runId, "status", "STOPPING", "activeWorker", true,
170:                "message", "Stop requested. Delete Data will unlock after the replay worker has fully stopped.");
171:    }
172:
173:    public synchronized long start(RegressionTestRunRequest request) {
174:        return start(request, ReplayDataSource.DATABASE);
175:    }
176:
177:    /** FIX-11H explicit replay-only OLD/NEW selector. Production is not configurable through this mode. */
178:    public synchronized long start(RegressionTestRunRequest request, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
179:        if (request == null || request.symbol() == null || request.symbol().isBlank()
180:                || request.startTime() == null || request.endTime() == null
181:                || !request.endTime().isAfter(request.startTime())) {
182:            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol, start time and end time are required, and end must be after start.");
183:        }
184:
185:        Integer activeRuns = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("""
186:                SELECT COUNT(*)
187:                FROM analysis_test_run
188:                WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
189:                """, Integer.class);
190:        if (activeRuns != null && activeRuns > 0) {
191:            Map<String, Object> active = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap("""
192:                    SELECT id, test_name, symbol, status, progress_percent, started_at, created_at
193:                    FROM analysis_test_run
194:                    WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
195:                    ORDER BY id DESC
196:                    LIMIT 1
197:                    """);
198:            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
199:                    "Regression test #" + active.get("id") + " is already " + active.get("status")
200:                            + " for " + active.get("symbol") + ". Wait for it to finish before starting another run.");
201:        }
202:
203:        String symbol = request.symbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
204:        ReplayDataSource effectiveSource = dataSource == null ? ReplayDataSource.DATABASE : dataSource;
205:        String baseTestName = request.testName() == null || request.testName().isBlank()
206:                ? symbol + " regression" : request.testName().trim();
207:        String testName = (baseTestName + " [" + effectiveSource.name() + "]").substring(
208:                0, Math.min(180, baseTestName.length() + effectiveSource.name().length() + 3));
209:
210:        KeyHolder keyHolder = new GeneratedKeyHolder();
211:        jdbcTemplate.update(connection -> {
212:            PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement("""
213:                    INSERT INTO analysis_test_run
214:                        (test_name, symbol, start_time, end_time, status, progress_percent, current_step)
215:                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'PENDING', 0, 'Queued from Administration')
216:                    """, Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS);
217:            statement.setString(1, testName);
218:            statement.setString(2, symbol);
219:            statement.setTimestamp(3, Timestamp.from(request.startTime()));
220:            statement.setTimestamp(4, Timestamp.from(request.endTime()));
221:            return statement;
222:        }, keyHolder);
223:
224:        Number key = keyHolder.getKey();
225:        if (key == null) {
226:            throw new IllegalStateException("Could not create regression test run.");
227:        }
228:        long id = key.longValue();
229:        // FIX-122: persist the business-rule revision separately from data-source/experimental modes.
230:        jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO fix122_replay_revision(test_run_id,enabled) VALUES (?,?)",
231:                id, !Boolean.FALSE.equals(request.fix122Enabled()));
232:        worker.runAsync(id, effectiveSource);
233:        return id;
234:    }

237:    /**
238:     * Archives one complete replay run before it can be cleared from the active test tables.
239:     *
240:     * IMPORTANT regression-history rule (introduced after the ETHUSDT Trade #2 investigation,
241:     * Aug-2026): test rows are evidence. We must be able to rerun changed production logic without
242:     * destroying the old pipeline/evidence/health/trade trace that still needs manual analysis.
243:     * Proven trades remain separate in proven_analyzed_trade and are never moved or deleted here.
244:     */
245:    @Transactional
246:    public synchronized Map<String, Object> archiveRun(long runId, String reason) {
247:        Integer active = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id=? AND status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')", Integer.class, runId);
248:        if (active != null && active > 0) {
249:            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Test #" + runId + " is still running and cannot be archived.");
250:        }
251:        List<Map<String,Object>> rows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id,test_name,symbol,start_time,end_time,status FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id=?", runId);
252:        if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Regression test #" + runId + " not found.");
253:        List<Map<String,Object>> existing = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id FROM regression_test_archive_batch WHERE source_test_run_id=?", runId);
254:        if (!existing.isEmpty()) return Map.of("archiveBatchId", existing.get(0).get("id"), "sourceTestRunId", runId, "alreadyArchived", true);
255:        Map<String,Object> r = rows.get(0);
256:        KeyHolder kh = new GeneratedKeyHolder();
257:        jdbcTemplate.update(c -> {
258:            PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement("INSERT INTO regression_test_archive_batch(source_test_run_id,test_name,symbol,start_time,end_time,source_status,archive_reason) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)", Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS);
259:            ps.setLong(1, runId); ps.setString(2, String.valueOf(r.get("test_name"))); ps.setString(3, String.valueOf(r.get("symbol")));
260:            ps.setTimestamp(4, (Timestamp) r.get("start_time")); ps.setTimestamp(5, (Timestamp) r.get("end_time"));
261:            ps.setString(6, String.valueOf(r.get("status"))); ps.setString(7, reason == null || reason.isBlank() ? "Archived before replay reset" : reason.trim());
262:            return ps;
263:        }, kh);
264:        long batchId = kh.getKey().longValue();
265:        int runs = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO analysis_test_run_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM analysis_test_run r WHERE r.id=?", batchId, runId);
266:        int signals = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM analysis_test_signal r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
267:        // FIX-069: archive the full production-shaped replay signal snapshot as well.
268:        int fullTradeSignals = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO trade_signal_test_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM trade_signal_test r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
269:        int opportunities = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO execution_opportunity_test_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM execution_opportunity_test r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
270:        int results = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO analysis_test_result_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM analysis_test_result r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
271:        int executions = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO wallet_execution_test_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM wallet_execution_test r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
272:        int positions = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO wallet_position_test_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM wallet_position_test r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
273:        int management = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO position_management_test_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM position_management_test r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
274:        int defensiveObservations = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
275:        int continuationGraceObservations = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO one_candle_continuation_grace_test_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM one_candle_continuation_grace_test r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
276:        return Map.ofEntries(
277:                Map.entry("archiveBatchId", batchId), Map.entry("sourceTestRunId", runId), Map.entry("alreadyArchived", false),
278:                Map.entry("runs", runs), Map.entry("signals", signals), Map.entry("tradeSignals", fullTradeSignals),
279:                Map.entry("opportunities", opportunities), Map.entry("results", results), Map.entry("executions", executions),
280:                Map.entry("positions", positions), Map.entry("management", management),
281:                Map.entry("defensiveRiskObservations", defensiveObservations),
282:                Map.entry("oneCandleContinuationGraceObservations", continuationGraceObservations));
283:    }

312:    @Transactional
313:    public synchronized Map<String, Object> resetAllTestData() {
314:        // FIX-090: Java worker ownership is authoritative. Database ERROR may be written a few
315:        // milliseconds before runAsync finally releases ownership; never purge during that gap.
316:        if (worker.hasActiveRuns()) {
317:            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
318:                    "A Replay/Test worker is still active. Stop it and wait for the worker to fully exit before Delete Data.");
319:        }
320:        Integer activeRuns = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("""
321:                SELECT COUNT(*) FROM analysis_test_run
322:                WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
323:                """, Integer.class);
324:        if (activeRuns != null && activeRuns > 0) {
325:            List<Map<String,Object>> liveRows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList(
326:                    "SELECT id,status FROM analysis_test_run WHERE status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')");
327:            boolean workerStillActive = liveRows.stream()
328:                    .anyMatch(r -> worker.isActive(((Number) r.get("id")).longValue()));
329:            if (workerStillActive) {
330:                throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
331:                        "A Replay/Test worker is still active. Use Stop Test and wait until it becomes ERROR before Delete Data.");
332:            }
333:            // Defensive cleanup for a stale DB status with no Java worker.
334:            jdbcTemplate.update("""
335:                    UPDATE analysis_test_run SET status='ERROR', current_step='Stopped/orphaned worker',
336:                        error_message='No active Replay/Test worker exists in this application instance.',
337:                        completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
338:                    WHERE status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')
339:                    """);
340:        }
341:
342:        // FIX-088: Delete Data is now a true purge, not an archive operation. Every replay/test
343:        // table that can feed Recent Test Runs, run detail, Shadow Trades, or archived replay
344:        // history is emptied. Persistent Proven records are deliberately excluded.
345:        int provenBefore = countRows("proven_analyzed_trade");
346:        int provenLegsBefore = countRows("proven_trade_leg_archive");
347:
348:        Map<String, Integer> deleted = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
349:        // Delete children before parents so the purge remains safe if foreign keys are added later.
350:        deleted.put("one_candle_continuation_grace_test", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM one_candle_continuation_grace_test"));
351:        deleted.put("defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test"));
352:        deleted.put("position_management_test", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM position_management_test"));
353:        deleted.put("wallet_execution_test", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM wallet_execution_test"));
354:        deleted.put("wallet_position_test", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM wallet_position_test"));
355:        deleted.put("execution_opportunity_test", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM execution_opportunity_test"));
356:        deleted.put("trade_signal_test", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM trade_signal_test"));
357:        deleted.put("analysis_test_signal", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM analysis_test_signal"));
358:        deleted.put("analysis_test_result", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM analysis_test_result"));
359:        // FIX-122: purge run-owned diagnostics before IDs can be reset; Production audit remains.
360:        deleted.put("fix122_evaluation", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id IS NOT NULL"));
361:        deleted.put("fix122_replay_revision", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM fix122_replay_revision"));
362:        deleted.put("analysis_test_run", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM analysis_test_run"));
363:
364:        deleted.put("one_candle_continuation_grace_test_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM one_candle_continuation_grace_test_archive"));
365:        deleted.put("defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test_archive"));
366:        deleted.put("position_management_test_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM position_management_test_archive"));
367:        deleted.put("wallet_execution_test_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM wallet_execution_test_archive"));
368:        deleted.put("wallet_position_test_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM wallet_position_test_archive"));
369:        deleted.put("execution_opportunity_test_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM execution_opportunity_test_archive"));
370:        deleted.put("trade_signal_test_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM trade_signal_test_archive"));
371:        deleted.put("analysis_test_signal_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM analysis_test_signal_archive"));
372:        deleted.put("analysis_test_result_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM analysis_test_result_archive"));
373:        deleted.put("analysis_test_run_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM analysis_test_run_archive"));
374:        deleted.put("regression_test_archive_batch", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM regression_test_archive_batch"));
375:
376:        // Validate the database state inside this same transaction. If any replay table still
377:        // contains rows, throw and rollback instead of reporting a false successful purge.
378:        java.util.List<String> replayTables = java.util.List.of(
379:                "one_candle_continuation_grace_test", "defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test", "position_management_test", "wallet_execution_test", "wallet_position_test",
380:                "execution_opportunity_test", "trade_signal_test", "analysis_test_signal",
381:                "analysis_test_result", "analysis_test_run",
382:                "one_candle_continuation_grace_test_archive", "defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test_archive", "position_management_test_archive", "wallet_execution_test_archive",
383:                "wallet_position_test_archive", "execution_opportunity_test_archive",
384:                "trade_signal_test_archive", "analysis_test_signal_archive",
385:                "analysis_test_result_archive", "analysis_test_run_archive",
386:                "regression_test_archive_batch");
387:        Map<String, Integer> remaining = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
388:        for (String table : replayTables) remaining.put(table, countRows(table));
389:        java.util.List<String> nonEmpty = remaining.entrySet().stream()
390:                .filter(e -> e.getValue() != 0).map(Map.Entry::getKey).toList();
391:        if (!nonEmpty.isEmpty()) {
392:            throw new IllegalStateException("Delete Test Data validation failed; rows remain in: " + String.join(", ", nonEmpty));
393:        }
394:
395:        int provenAfter = countRows("proven_analyzed_trade");
396:        int provenLegsAfter = countRows("proven_trade_leg_archive");
397:        if (provenBefore != provenAfter || provenLegsBefore != provenLegsAfter) {
398:            throw new IllegalStateException("Delete Test Data validation failed; Proven records changed unexpectedly.");
399:        }
400:
401:        // Reset only tables that own an AUTO_INCREMENT id. This is cosmetic and happens only
402:        // after successful delete + validation; Proven ids are never reset.
403:        java.util.List<String> autoIncrementTables = java.util.List.of(
404:                "one_candle_continuation_grace_test", "defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test", "wallet_execution_test", "wallet_position_test", "position_management_test",
405:                "execution_opportunity_test", "trade_signal_test", "analysis_test_signal",
406:                "analysis_test_result", "analysis_test_run", "regression_test_archive_batch");
407:        for (String table : autoIncrementTables) jdbcTemplate.execute("ALTER TABLE " + table + " AUTO_INCREMENT = 1");
408:
409:        return Map.of(
410:                "success", true,
411:                "message", "All replay/test data deleted and validated; Proven trades preserved.",
412:                "deleted", deleted,
413:                "remaining", remaining,
414:                "provenTradesPreserved", provenAfter,
415:                "provenTradeLegsPreserved", provenLegsAfter
416:        );
417:    }
418:
419:    private int countRows(String table) {
420:        Integer count = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM " + table, Integer.class);
421:        return count == null ? 0 : count;
422:    }

457:    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
458:    public List<Map<String, Object>> latestRuns() {
459:        List<Map<String, Object>> runs = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
460:                SELECT r.id, r.test_name, r.symbol, r.start_time, r.end_time, r.status, r.progress_percent,
461:                       r.current_step, r.heartbeat_at, r.started_at, r.completed_at, r.created_at,
462:                       r.timing_total_ns,
463:                       (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM wallet_position_test w WHERE w.test_run_id=r.id AND w.exit_time IS NOT NULL) AS closed_trade_count,
464:                       (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM proven_analyzed_trade p WHERE p.source_test_run_id=r.id) AS proven_trade_count
465:                FROM analysis_test_run r
466:                ORDER BY r.id DESC
467:                LIMIT 20
468:                """);
469:        // FIX-087: heartbeat freshness is only a persistence signal; actual worker ownership is
470:        // surfaced separately so a long healthy replay is not presented as resumable.
471:        runs.forEach(run -> run.put("active_worker", worker.isActive(((Number) run.get("id")).longValue())));
472:        return runs;
473:    }

476:    public List<Map<String, Object>> signals(long runId) {

490:    public List<Map<String, Object>> trades(long runId) {

492:                SELECT id, test_run_id, symbol, entry_time, entry_price, exit_time, exit_price, exit_reason,
493:                       realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent, position_percent, status,
494:                       EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM proven_analyzed_trade p WHERE p.source_test_run_id = wallet_position_test.test_run_id AND p.source_trade_id = wallet_position_test.id) AS proven_success
495:                FROM wallet_position_test

497:                ORDER BY entry_time ASC
498:                LIMIT 500

504:
505:    /**
506:     * FIX-11S: one read-only chart payload for an entire Replay run. Replay executions are
507:     * taken from the isolated wallet_execution_test rows; Production executions are only
508:     * queried for the same symbol and requested Replay window. An empty Production result
509:     * is a normal case and is deliberately returned as [] rather than null.
510:     */
511:    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
512:    public Map<String, Object> allTradesChart(long runId, String interval) {
513:        List<Map<String, Object>> runs = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
514:                SELECT id, symbol, start_time, end_time
515:                FROM analysis_test_run
516:                WHERE id = ?
517:                """, runId);
518:        if (runs.isEmpty()) {
519:            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Replay run not found.");
520:        }
521:
522:        Map<String, Object> run = runs.get(0);
523:        String symbol = String.valueOf(run.get("symbol")).trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
524:        Timestamp start = (Timestamp) run.get("start_time");
525:        Timestamp end = (Timestamp) run.get("end_time");
526:        String normalizedInterval = switch (interval == null ? "5m" : interval.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {
527:            case "1m", "5m", "1h", "4h" -> interval.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
528:            default -> "5m";
529:        };
530:
531:        List<Map<String, Object>> candles = candleReader(jdbcTemplate).queryForList("""
532:                SELECT open_time, open_price, high_price, low_price, close_price, volume
533:                FROM candle
534:                WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND open_time BETWEEN ? AND ?
535:                ORDER BY open_time ASC
536:                """, symbol, normalizedInterval, start, end);
537:
538:        List<Map<String, Object>> replayExecutions = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
539:                SELECT id, side, execution_time, execution_price, quantity, notional_usdt,
540:                       position_percent, signal_interval, signal_decision, execution_source,
541:                       execution_code, execution_reason, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent
542:                FROM wallet_execution_test
543:                WHERE test_run_id=? AND symbol=?
544:                  AND execution_time BETWEEN ? AND ?
545:                  AND UPPER(side) IN ('BUY','SELL')
546:                ORDER BY execution_time ASC, id ASC
547:                """, runId, symbol, start, end);
548:
549:        List<Map<String, Object>> productionExecutions = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
550:                SELECT id, signal_id, side, executed_at AS execution_time, price_usdt AS execution_price,
551:                       quantity, gross_amount_usdt, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent,
552:                       execution_type, execution_reason, execution_message
553:                FROM wallet_trade
554:                WHERE symbol=? AND status='EXECUTED'
555:                  AND executed_at BETWEEN ? AND ?
556:                  AND UPPER(side) IN ('BUY','SELL')
557:                ORDER BY executed_at ASC, id ASC
558:                """, symbol, start, end);
559:
560:        Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
561:        result.put("runId", runId);
562:        result.put("symbol", symbol);
563:        result.put("interval", normalizedInterval);
564:        result.put("from", start);
565:        result.put("to", end);
566:        result.put("candles", candles);

656:     * Archive only one reviewed execution leg. This is intentionally separate from
657:     * regression-run archiving: a reviewer can preserve the BUY or SELL point on its
658:     * own without freezing/copying the complete test run. Clear Data still keeps the
659:     * existing full-run safety archive so replay diagnostics remain recoverable.

661:    @Transactional
662:    public Map<String, Object> archiveProvenTradeLeg(long provenTradeId, String requestedSide) {
663:        String side = requestedSide == null ? "" : requestedSide.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
664:        if (!side.equals("BUY") && !side.equals("SELL")) {
665:            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Archive side must be BUY or SELL.");
666:        }
667:        List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
668:                SELECT id, source_test_run_id, source_trade_id, symbol, entry_time, entry_price, exit_time, exit_price,
669:                       exit_reason, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent
670:                FROM proven_analyzed_trade
671:                WHERE id=?
672:                """, provenTradeId);
673:        if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Proven trade not found.");
674:        Map<String, Object> trade = rows.get(0);
675:        Object time = side.equals("BUY") ? trade.get("entry_time") : trade.get("exit_time");
676:        Object price = side.equals("BUY") ? trade.get("entry_price") : trade.get("exit_price");
677:        if (time == null || price == null) {
678:            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, side + " leg is not available for this proven trade.");
679:        }
680:        jdbcTemplate.update("""
681:                INSERT INTO proven_trade_leg_archive
682:                    (proven_trade_id, source_test_run_id, source_trade_id, symbol, side, execution_time, execution_price,
683:                     exit_reason, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent)
684:                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
685:                ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE execution_time=VALUES(execution_time), execution_price=VALUES(execution_price),
686:                    exit_reason=VALUES(exit_reason), realized_pnl_usdt=VALUES(realized_pnl_usdt),
687:                    realized_pnl_percent=VALUES(realized_pnl_percent), archived_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
688:                """, provenTradeId, trade.get("source_test_run_id"), trade.get("source_trade_id"), trade.get("symbol"),
689:                side, time, price, side.equals("SELL") ? trade.get("exit_reason") : null,
690:                side.equals("SELL") ? trade.get("realized_pnl_usdt") : null,
691:                side.equals("SELL") ? trade.get("realized_pnl_percent") : null);
692:        return Map.of("archived", true, "provenTradeId", provenTradeId, "side", side);
693:    }

708:    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
709:    public Map<String, Object> provenTradeChart(String symbol, String interval) {
710:        String normalized = symbol == null ? "" : symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
711:        String normalizedInterval = switch (interval == null ? "5m" : interval.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {
712:            case "1m", "5m", "1h", "4h" -> interval.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
713:            default -> "5m";
714:        };
715:        List<Map<String, Object>> trades = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
716:                SELECT id, source_test_run_id, source_trade_id, symbol, entry_time, entry_price, exit_time, exit_price,
717:                       exit_reason, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent, position_percent, marked_at
718:                FROM proven_analyzed_trade
719:                WHERE symbol = ?
720:                ORDER BY entry_time ASC
721:                LIMIT 250
722:                """, normalized);
723:        java.util.LinkedHashMap<String, Map<String, Object>> candles = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
724:        for (Map<String, Object> trade : trades) {
725:            Timestamp entry = (Timestamp) trade.get("entry_time");
726:            Timestamp exit = (Timestamp) trade.get("exit_time");
727:            if (entry == null) continue;
728:            // Proven-analysis charts intentionally use real historical candles with a wide context window.
729:            // Keep seven hours before the BUY and seven hours after the SELL so manual review can
730:            // detect late entries and premature exits instead of judging only the replay window.
731:            Instant from = entry.toInstant().minus(java.time.Duration.ofHours(7));
732:            Instant to = (exit == null ? entry.toInstant() : exit.toInstant()).plus(java.time.Duration.ofHours(7));
733:            List<Map<String, Object>> segment = candleReader(jdbcTemplate).queryForList("""
734:                    SELECT open_time, open_price, high_price, low_price, close_price, volume
735:                    FROM candle
736:                    WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND open_time BETWEEN ? AND ?
737:                    ORDER BY open_time ASC
738:                    """, normalized, normalizedInterval, Timestamp.from(from), Timestamp.from(to));
739:            for (Map<String, Object> c : segment) candles.put(String.valueOf(c.get("open_time")), c);
740:        }
741:        return Map.of("symbol", normalized, "interval", normalizedInterval, "trades", trades,
742:                "candles", new java.util.ArrayList<>(candles.values()));
743:    }

128:     * REST backfill it can never insert an absent identity and it never makes a
129:     * runtime close token.  This keeps a late recovery from stealing a normal
130:     * live close admission.
131:     */
132:    public boolean finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
133:        BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
134:        BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,

140:              closed=1,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)

142:              AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')
143:            """, Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,
144:            numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime)) == 1;
145:    }

169:    private int write(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
170:        BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,

### src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java 465-700
465:     * FIX-11H: loads one immutable replay dataset. The prefix comes from the exact existing
466:     * closed/as-of query, so missing candle gaps cannot shorten history. The forward segment
467:     * explicitly requires closed=true. No Production caller reaches this method.
468:     */
469:    private ReplayDataset loadDataset(String symbol, String btcSymbol, Instant start, Instant end) {
470:        Map<ReplayDataset.CandleKey, List<Candle>> candleMap = new HashMap<>();
471:        Set<String> symbols = new LinkedHashSet<>(List.of(symbol, btcSymbol));
472:        int historyLimit = technicalIndicatorService.regressionHistoryLimit();
473:        for (String sym : symbols) {
474:            for (String interval : List.of("1m", "5m", "1h")) {
475:                List<Candle> prefix = new java.util.ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
476:                        sym, interval, start, PageRequest.of(0, historyLimit)));
477:                java.util.Collections.reverse(prefix);
478:                List<Candle> forward = candleRepository
479:                        .findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
480:                                sym, interval, start, end);
481:                List<Candle> merged = new java.util.ArrayList<>(prefix.size() + forward.size());
482:                merged.addAll(prefix);
483:                Instant prefixEnd = prefix.isEmpty() ? Instant.EPOCH : prefix.get(prefix.size() - 1).getOpenTime();
484:                forward.stream().filter(c -> c.getOpenTime().isAfter(prefixEnd)).forEach(merged::add);
485:                candleMap.put(new ReplayDataset.CandleKey(sym, interval), merged);
486:                log.info("FIX-11H dataset candles: symbol={}, interval={}, prefix={}, forward={}, merged={}",
487:                        sym, interval, prefix.size(), forward.size(), merged.size());
488:            }
489:        }
490:
491:        Map<ReplayDataset.SignalKey, Long> lineage = new HashMap<>();
492:        // FIX-11H / FIX-112D: lineage is candle identity, not signal persistence timing.
493:        // Recovery/backfilled Production signals can be generated hours after their candle, so
494:        // generatedAt MUST NOT define this preload window. Select and match lineage by the same
495:        // exact candleOpenTime identity used by ReplayDataset.sourceSignalId().
496:        for (TradeSignal signal : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndCandleOpenTimeBetweenOrderByCandleOpenTimeAsc(symbol, start, end)) {
497:            ReplayDataset.SignalKey key = new ReplayDataset.SignalKey(
498:                    signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(), signal.getCandleOpenTime());
499:            Long previous = lineage.putIfAbsent(key, signal.getId());
500:            if (previous != null && !previous.equals(signal.getId())) {
501:                throw new IllegalStateException("Replay lineage invariant violated for " + key
502:                        + ": signalIds=" + previous + "," + signal.getId());
503:            }
504:        }
505:        log.info("FIX-11H dataset lineage: symbol={}, entries={}", symbol, lineage.size());
506:        return new ReplayDataset(candleMap, lineage);
507:    }

514:    private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
515:                                      int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
516:        int resolved = 0;
517:        int size = candles.size();
518:        for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
519:            checkStopRequested(runId);
520:            Candle candle = candles.get(i);
521:            List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
522:                    symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
523:            if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
524:                resolved++;
525:            }
526:            if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
527:                int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
528:                updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,
529:                        "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
530:            }
531:        }
532:        return resolved;
533:    }

134:    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
135:    public Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> calculateSnapshotForRegression(
136:            String symbol, String intervalCode, Instant candleOpenTime, ReplayDataset dataset) {
137:        if (candleOpenTime == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression candle open time is required");
138:        String normalisedSymbol = normaliseSymbol(symbol);
139:        String normalisedInterval = normaliseInterval(intervalCode);

142:        if (candles.size() < MINIMUM_CANDLES) return Optional.empty();
143:        Collections.reverse(candles);
144:        return Optional.of(calculateSnapshot(normalisedSymbol, normalisedInterval, candles));
145:    }

The working tree confirms the key Replay input distinction: its execution timeline accepts all candles, while its indicator snapshots use only closed candles. I’m now tracing the higher-timeframe service’s callers and its tests to separate a documented design contradiction from unverified deployment claims.

181:## Pending: FIX-154 higher-timeframe BUY validation
182-
183:FIX-154 is separate from the local-candle change and is not implemented.
184-
185-Its purpose is to let a historical bearish 1h signal affect a current 1m BUY
186:only when the historical evidence is fresh, quality-valid, and sufficiently
187-strong. `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` on a historical signal is investigatory
188:evidence, not a higher-timeframe quality gate; the current 1m BTC protection
189-remains unchanged.
190-

257-`HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService` is the single pure assessment boundary for

259:entities, so a SOFT 1h conflict can be assessed as effective `NEUTRAL` without

264-the legacy 5m bearish veto keeps priority.

276:## FIX-153 automatic local-candle reconciliation — enabled 2026-10-10
277-
278-In `shared-market.mode=OFF`, `LocalCandleReconciliationService` runs every
279-five seconds on its dedicated single-worker executor. It finds eligible
280:missing or interrupted candle identities inside the data-quality window, reads
281-the authoritative Binance candle, and writes only verified final evidence.
282-An existing open row is finalized by an update-only path; a missing row uses a
283-plain insert-if-absent backfill path. Neither route creates a close token,
284-dispatches analysis, or executes a trade. The next genuine live close resumes
285:ordinary analysis after the quality window is complete.

3-Date: 2026-10-10  
4:Status: implemented in the local working tree; not deployed.

6-## Scope
7-
8:FIX-154 changes only higher-timeframe BUY authority. It does not enable or
9:change FIX-153 reconciliation/runtime behavior.
10-
11-The objective is to distinguish trusted, strong bearish 1h evidence from
12:unknown or weaker evidence without changing a stored historical signal.

14-## Implemented policy
--
16-1. The current 1m BUY still requires fresh 5m and 1h context.
17-2. A bearish 5m returns the existing `5M_BEARISH_VETO` first.
18:3. A bearish 1h with `data_quality_valid` `NULL` or `false` returns
19-   `1H_BEARISH_VETO`.
20-4. A trusted 1h `STRONG_SELL` with `confidence_score >= 70` returns
21-   `HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO`.
22-5. A trusted 1h `SELL`, or `STRONG_SELL` with confidence below 70, is a
23:   SOFT conflict. It passes effective 1h `NEUTRAL` to profile logic and caps
24-   every allowed initial entry at 25%.
25:6. A non-bearish 1h with unknown quality keeps its raw decision; FIX-154 does
26:   not tighten bullish/non-bearish authority merely because historical quality
27-   is absent.
28:7. Existing progressive/add logic retains its raw 1h requirement. A SOFT
29:   conflict cannot authorize an add. Optional `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` evidence
30-   is observational only.

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The supplied review question is only `Perform`; it identifies neither an artifact nor an acceptance criterion. An approval/rejection cannot be tied to a defined subject.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** FIX-154 implementation code and focused tests exist in the local working tree, but that does not prove deployment. `AGENTS.md` and older portions of `codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` say it is pending, while later architecture/changelog/investigation entries describe an implementation and, once, “deployment.” These records conflict.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The current FIX-154 code conflicts with the pending rules in `AGENTS.md`:
  - Unknown/invalid-quality bearish 1h evidence rejects the BUY.
  - A quality-valid `STRONG_SELL` 1h at confidence ≥70 rejects even when the fresh 5m is non-bearish; bearish 5m confirmation is not required.
  - No recovery-specific path exists; a trusted soft 1h simply permits a capped entry when 5m is non-bearish.
  The focused tests explicitly assert these behaviours.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Claude’s Replay finality concern is substantiated and sharper than initially stated: the default Replay mode is `DATABASE`; its timeline admits `closed=0` candles, while snapshot calculation reads only closed candles. Such a row can cause a later evaluation of the preceding closed-candle identity. `analysis_test_signal` records the open candle identity, whereas `trade_signal_test` follows the generated signal’s preceding closed-candle identity. This can inflate or misattribute Replay output without using future candle data.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The direct Binance ingestion path only publishes `CandleClosedEvent` after a persisted open-to-closed transition. Therefore this Replay behaviour is not equivalent to that production close-transition path.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The documented confidence threshold of 70 is a policy boundary, not demonstrated predictive validation. A count of signals above/below 70 does not establish outcomes, false-veto rate, slippage/fees impact, or out-of-sample robustness.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The earlier FIX-122 objection should remain withdrawn: Production reads `trading.fix122.enabled`; Replay substitutes its run-local setting only while its scope is active.

## OBJECTIONS

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** No review may be approved while its subject remains `Perform`.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No document is currently safe to treat as authoritative for FIX-154’s intended trading rule; `AGENTS.md`, the current code, and project documentation disagree materially.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Default database Replay has a finality/identity-parity defect. Any claim that its signal counts or candle lineage match production is unsupported until this is addressed and regression-tested.
- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The 70 threshold must not be represented as performance-validated without an appropriately controlled outcome analysis that includes transaction costs, slippage, selection bias, and out-of-sample evidence.

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Whether FIX-154 was deployed cannot be established from this working tree or its documentation; no production access was used.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Whether V107’s current checksum matches the database-recorded checksum was not verified.
- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The operational frequency and realised impact of unclosed Replay rows require bounded database evidence or deterministic tests.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No immutable source baseline exists for this review: the working tree contains extensive modified and untracked files.
